                <div n="32" type="adhyāya">
                    <milestone facs="dscn3040 fol 064.jpg lower" unit="jpeg"/>

                    <pb facs="66" n="65r, line 4"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.32.1"><l> athātaḥ svabhāvavipratipattiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.32.3"><l> svabhāvaprasiddhānāṃ śarīraikadeśānām
                        anyathātvaṃ maraṇāya | tad yathā śuklānāṃ kṛṣṇatvaṃ kṛṣṇānāṃ
                        śuklatvaṃ | sthirāṇām mṛdutvaṃ | calānām acalatvam acalā<milestone unit="jpeg" facs="dscn3041 fol 065.jpg upper"/><pb facs="67" n="65v"/><lb n="1"/>nāñ calatvaṃ | pṛthūnāṃ saṃkṣiptatvaṃ
                        saṃkṣiptānāṃ pṛthutvaṃ | dīrghāṇāṃ hrasvatvaṃ ca hrasvānāṃ
                        dīrghatā | apatanadharmmiṇāṃ patanam akasmāc
                        chaityoṣṇasneharaukṣyaprastambhavaivarṇṇyāni apasarppaṇa<g ref="#newa-gap-filler"/><lb n="2"/>m aṅgānāṃ </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.32.4"><l> svebhyaḥ sthānebhyaḥ śarīraikadeśānām
                        avasraṃsotkṣiptabhrā<cb/>ntapatitavimuktanirggamātigamagurulaghutvāni
                        pravālavarṇṇavyaṅgaprādurbbhāvo vākasmāt sirāṇāṃ <lb n="3"/>ca
                        darśana lalāṭe nāsāvaṃśe vā piṭakotpattir udakotpattir
                        nne<cb/>trarogam vināsrupravṛttir lalāṭe vā prabhātakāle
                        svedapravṛttiḥ | gomayacūrṇṇaprakāśasya rajaso da<lb n="4"/>rśanam
                        uttamāṅge līyanam vā kapotakaṅkagṛddhraprabhṛtīnāṃ
                        mūtra<cb/>purīṣapravṛddhir abhuñjanānāṃ stanamūlahṛdayorassu ca
                        śūlotpattayaḥ | madhye śūnatvam anteṣu pa<g ref="#newa-gap-filler"/><lb n="5"/>rimlāyitvaṃ | viparyayo vā
                        naṣṭahīnavikalavihṛtasvaraṃ vā vivarṇṇapuṣpaprādurbbhāvo vā
                        dantanakhaśarīreṣu puṣpadarśanaṃ yasya cāpsu kaphaśakṛdretāṃsi
                        nimajjanti yasya <milestone unit="jpeg" facs="dscn3041 fol 065.jpg lower"/><pb facs="67" n="66r"/><lb n="1"/>ca dṛṣṭimaṇḍale bhinnavikṛtāni rūpāṇy ālocyante
                        snehābhyaktakeśāṅga iva yo bhāti yaś ca durbbalo
                        bhaktadveṣātisārābhyām pīḍyate | kāsamānaś ca tṛṣṇātibhūtaḥ kṣīṇaś
                            cha<g ref="#newa-gap-filler"/><lb n="2"/>rddibhaktadeśayuktaḥ
                        saphenarudhirodvāmī hatasvaraḥ śūlābhi<cb/>hataś ca
                        manuṣyaśūnakaracaraṇo nnadveṣī srastapiṇḍakāṃsapāṇipādaḥ | yaś ca
                        pūrvāhṇe bhu<lb n="3"/>ktam aparāhṇe ścharddayaty avidagdhaṃ
                        sāryate vā jvarakāsābhibhūtaḥ <cb/>sa śvāsān mriyate | vastavad
                        vilapamāno bhūmau patati srastamuṣkaḥ stabdhaśepo bhagnagrīvaḥ
                            praṇa<lb n="4"/>ṣṭamehaś ca manuṣyaḥ prāgviśuṣyamāṇahṛdaya
                        ārddraśarīraḥ | <cb/>yaś ca loṣṭaṃ loṣṭenābhihanti | kāṣṭham vā
                        kāṣṭhena tṛṇāni vā cchinnanti | adharoṣṭham vā daśati utta<lb n="5"/>roṣṭham vā leḍhi | āluñcati karṇṇau keśām̐ś ca |
                            devadvijaguru<g ref="#newa-gap-filler"/>suhṛdvaidyān vā
                        vidveṣṭi yasya ca vakrānuvakragā grahā garhitasthānagatāḥ |
                        janmaṛkṣam vāsyolkāśa<lb n="6"/>nibhyām abhihanyate | rātrau vā
                        gṛhadvāraśayanāśanayānavāhanamaṇiratnopakaraṇagarhitalakṣaṇanimittaprādurbbhāvo
                        veti || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.32.5"><l>
                        <abbr type="suspension">bha</abbr><ex cert="medium">vanti
                            cātra</ex> ||
                        <caesura/>
                         cikitsyamānaḥ samyak tu vikāro <milestone unit="jpeg" facs="dscn3042 fol 066.jpg upper"/><pb facs="68" n="66v"/><lb n="1"/>yo bhivarddhate |
                        <caesura/>
                         prakṣīṇabala<pc> </pc>māṃsasya lakṣaṇan tadgatāyuṣaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.32.6"><l> nivarttate mahāvyādhiḥ sahasā yasya dehinaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         na cāhāraphalaṃ yasya dṛśyate sa vinaśyati || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.32.7"><l> etāni riṣṭarūpāṇi<g ref="#newa-gap-filler"/>
                        <lb n="2"/>samyag budhyeta yo bhiṣak |
                        <caesura/>
                         sādhyāsādhyaparīkṣañ ca sa rājñaḥ sa<cb/>mmato bhaved iti ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.32.trailer"><l> 31 || ❈ || </l></lg>

                <div n="33" type="adhyāya">

                    <milestone facs="dscn3042 fol 066.jpg upper" n="66r2" unit="folio"/>

                    <!--  
 166r, line 2, column 2 
 -->

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.1"><l> athāto vāraṇīyam adhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.3"><l> upadravais tu ye juṣṭā-<lb n="3"/>vyādhayo
                        yānty avāryatāṃ | rasāyanair vvinā vatsa tāṃ śṛṇv ekamanā <cb n="2"/>mama || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.4"><l> vātavyādhiḥ pramehaś ca kuṣṭhānyatha
                        bhagandaraṃ | arśośmarī mūḍhagarbho bhavaty udaram aṣṭamam <lb n="4"/>|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.5"><l> aṣṭāv ete mahāntaḥ syūr vvyādhayo dustarāḥ sadā
                        | prāṇamāṃsakṣa<cb n="2"/>yaḥ śophas tṛṣṇā ccharddir jvaras tathā
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.6"><l> atīsāraś ca mūrcchā ca
                        <caesura/>
                         hikkāśvāsas tathaiva ca | etair upa<lb n="5"/>dravair jjuṣṭān
                        sarvvān etān vivarjayet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.7"><l> śūnaṃ suptatvacam bhagnaṅ ka<cb n="2"/>mpādhmānanipīḍitaṃ | rujārttam antañ ca naram vātavyādhir
                        vvināśayet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.8"><l> yathoktopadravāvi<lb n="6"/>ṣṭam atiprasrutam
                        eva ca | piṭakāpīḍitaṃ gāḍhaṃ prameho hanti mānavaṃ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.9"><l> prabhinnaṃ prasrutāṅgañ ca raktanetraṃ
                        hatasvaraṃ | pañcakarmmaguṇātītaṃ kuṣṭhaṃ hanti hi
                            kuṣṭhi<milestone unit="jpeg" facs="dscn3042 fol 066.jpg                             lower"/><pb facs="68" n="67r"/><lb n="1"/>naṃ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.11"><l> vātamūtrapurīṣāṇi krimayaḥ śukram eva ca |
                        bhagandarāt prasravanti sa naśyati bhagandarī || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.10"><l>
                        <!--  
  
                            the manuscript switches the vulgate 's 10 and 11, as well as 13 and 14
                        
 --> tṛṣṇarocakaśūlārtta<lb n="2"/>m atiprasrutaśoṇitaṃ |
                        śophātīsārasaṃyuktaṃ durnāmā kṣapayen na<cb n="2"/>raṃ ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.12"><l> praśūnanābhivṛṣaṇaṃ vaddhamūtrarujāturaṃ |
                        <caesura/>
                         aśmarī kṣapayaty āśu sikatāśarkkarānvitaṃ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.14"><l> pā<lb n="3"/>rśvabhaṅgānnavidveṣaḥ
                        śophātīsārapīḍitam |
                        <caesura/>
                         vivarjayed udariṇa<cb n="2"/>m virikto yo 'bhipūryate || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.13"><l>yonīsamvaraṇaṃ saṅgaḥ kukṣo makkallasaṃjñitaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         hanyuḥ striyaṃ mūḍhaga-<lb n="4"/>rvbhe yathoktāś cāpy upadravāḥ
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.15"><l> visaṃjñas tām yate yas tu śete nipati-<cb n="2"/>to yathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         śītārddito 'ntaruṣṇaś ca jvareṇa mriyate naraḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.16"><l> yo hṛṣṭaromā raktākṣo hṛdi saṃghātaśūla<lb n="5"/>vān |
                        <caesura/>
                         vaktreṇa cocchvasiti taṃ jvaro hanti mānavaṃ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.17"><l> hikkāśvāsa samā<cb n="2"/>yuktaṃ mūḍham
                        vibhrāntalocanaṃ |
                        <caesura/>
                         santatocchvāsinaṃ kṣīṇaṃ naraṃ kṣapayate jvaraḥ || </l></lg>



                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.33.19"><l> śvāsaśūlapipāsārtaṃ-<lb n="7"/>kṣīṇañ
                        jvaranipīḍitaṃ |
                        <caesura/>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.39.3"><l>madanakuṭajajīmūtake<lb n="4"/>kṣvākudhāmārggavakṛtavedhanalodhrasarṣapaviḍaṅgapippalīkara<cb/>ñjaprapūnāḍakovidārāriṣṭāśvagandhāvidulabandhumadhukajīvantī
                            bimbīphalāmṛgervvāru citra<lb n="5"/>svetā
                            śalapuṣpīvacācetyūrddhvabhāgaharāṇi || tatra
                            kovidārapūrvvā<cb/>ṇāmphalāni | kovidārādīnām mūlāni ||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.39.4"><l>tṛvṛcchyāmādantīdravantīsaphalāviṣāṇigavākṣī<lb n="6"/>cchagalāntrīpīlusnehāsuvarṇṇakṣīrīcitrakakiṇihīkuśakāsatilvakampilyakarampakapāṭalā
                                <milestone unit="jpeg" facs="dscn3055 fol 079.jpg upper"/><pb facs="81" n="79v"/><lb n="1"/>pūgaharītakyāmalakavibhītakanīlinīpañcaṅgulapūtīkāragvadhamahāvṛkṣasaptacchadārkkareṇu
                            jyotiṣmatī cetyadhobhāgaharāṇi ||<lb n="2"/>tatra
                            tilvakapūrvvāṇāṃ mūlāni | tilvakādīnām pāṭalyantānāñ ca <cb/>
                            tvacaḥ | pūgādīnām eraṇḍāntānām phalāni | pūtīkāragvadhayoḥ
                            patrāṇi | śeṣāṇāṃ kṣīrāṇīti ||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.39.5"><l>ko<lb n="3"/>śātakī saptalā devadālī
                            karavallikā cety ubhayato bhāgaharā<cb/>ṇi | eṣāṃ svarasāḥ
                            ||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.39.6"><l>pippalīviḍaṅgāpāmārggaśigruśirīṣasiddhārthakamaricakaravīrabimbīgirika<lb n="4"/>rṇṇikākiṇihīvacājyotiṣmatīkarañjārkkalaśunātiviṣāśṛ<cb/>

                            ambupṛthivīguṇabhūyiṣṭhāni | vamanadravyāṇy
                            agnivāyuguṇabhūyiṣṭhāni | agnivāyū hi laghū laghutvāc
                            corddhvam uttiṣṭhataḥ | tasmād vamana<lb n="2"/>prāmāṇyād
                            agnivāyubhūyiṣṭhāny ubhayaguṇabhūyiṣṭhan dravyam ubha<cb/>yato
                            bhāgaharaṃ | ākāśaguṇabhūyiṣṭhaṃ saṃśamanaṃ | sāṃgrāhikam
                            anilaguṇabhūyiṣṭhaṃ | dīpa<lb n="3"/>nam agniguṇabhūyiṣṭhaṃ |
                            lekhanam anilānalaguṇabhūyiṣṭhaṃ | <cb/>bṛṃhana<metamark rend="line-above-na"/>m pṛthivyambuguṇabhūyiṣṭhaṃ | evam
                            auṣadhakarmmāṇy anumānāt sādhayet || </l></lg>


                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.41.7"><l>bhavanti ślokāḥ ||
                            <caesura/>
                            <lb n="4"/>mahyambvagnyātmakair ddravyais tribhiḥ sāmyati
                            mārutaḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             khabhūmyambu<cb/>vāyujaiḥ pittañ caturbbhiḥ saṃpraśāmyati || </l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.41.8"><l> kaphaḥ khatejo'nilajais tribhiḥ śāmyati
                            dehināṃ |
                            <caesura/>
                            <lb n="6"/>khavāyuktābhyān dravyābhyāṃ vṛddhim abhyeti mārutaḥ
                            || </l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.41.9"><l> āgneyam eva <cb/>yad dravyan tena pittam
                            udīryate |
                            <caesura/>
                             mahyambujābhyān dravyābhyāṅ kaphaś cābhivivarddhate || </l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.41.10"><l> evam eva guṇādhi<g ref="#newa-gap-filler"/><lb n="6"/>kyaṃ dravye dravye vyavasthitaṃ |
                            <caesura/>
                             dviśo vā bahuśo vāpi jñātvā doṣe 'vacācaret || </l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.41.11"><l> tatra ya ime guṇāṣṭauvīryasaṃjñakāḥ |
                            śītoṣṇasnigdharūkṣamṛdutīkṣṇapicchilaviṣadās teṣān
                                tīkṣṇo<milestone unit="jpeg" facs="dscn3060 fol 084.jpg upper"/><pb facs="86" n="84v"/><lb n="1"/>ṣṇāv āgneyau | śītapicchilāv
                            ambuguṇabhūyiṣṭhau | pṛthivīsomaguṇabhūyiṣṭhaḥ | snehas
                            toyākāśaguṇabhūyiṣṭhaṃ | mṛdutvaṃ | vāyuguṇabhūyiṣṭhaṃ
                            raukṣyaṃ | kṣitisamīraṇabhūyiṣṭhaṃ vai<g ref="#newa-gap-filler"/><lb n="2"/>ṣadyaṃ | vipākāv
                            uktaguṇau | karmmāṇy uṣṇasya
                            dahanapācanamū<cb/>rcchanasvedanavamanavirecanāni || śītasya
                                prahlādanaviṣkambhanasthirīkaraṇaprasādanakledana<lb n="3"/>jīvanāni | snigdhasya
                            snehanabṛṃhaṇasantarppaṇavyājīkaraṇa<cb/>vayaḥsthāpanāni ||
                            rūkṣasyānilavṛddhisaṃgrahaṇapīḍanavirūkṣaśoṣarohaṇāni ||
                                viṣada<g ref="#newa-gap-filler"/><lb n="4"/>sya
                            kledāvūṣaṇavirūkṣaśoṣarohaṇāni || picchilasyopa<g ref="#newa-gap-filler"/><cb/>lepanapūraṇabṛṃhaṇasaṃśleṣaṇavyājīkaraṇāni || mṛdo
                            raktamān saprasādanamukhyasaṃspa<g ref="#newa-gap-filler"/><lb n="5"/>rśanāni || tīkṣṇasya
                            saṅgrahācūṣaṇāvadāraṇaśrāvaṇāni | <cb/>tatroṣṇasnigdhau
                            vātaghnau | śītamṛdupicchilāḥ pittaghnāḥ | tīkṣṇarūkṣaviṣadāḥ
                            śleṣmaghnāḥ | guru<lb n="6"/>pāko vātapittaghn<subst><del rend="erased"/>
                                <add>a</add></subst>ḥ | laghupākaḥ śleṣmapittaghna iti |
                            teṣu mṛduśītoṣṇāḥ sparśagrāhyāḥ | picchilaviṣadau
                            cakṣuḥsparśābhyāṃ | snigdharūkṣau cakṣuṣā | tīkṣṇaḥ
                                sukhaduḥ<milestone unit="jpeg" facs="dscn3060 fol 084.jpg                             lower"/><pb facs="86" n="85r"/><lb n="1"/>khotpādanāt |
                            gurupākaḥ sṛṣṭaviṇmūtratayā kaphā<unclear reason="eccentric_ductus">ṃ</unclear> kleśanañ ca | laghur
                            bbaddhaviṇmūtratayā mārutakopanañ ca | tatra tulyaguṇeṣu
                            bhūteṣu rasavaiśeṣyam upalakṣayet ||<g ref="#newa-placeholder-mark"/>
                            <lb n="2"/>yathā madhuro guruś ca pārthivaḥ |
                            madhurasnigdhaśītāś cāpyā iti || </l></lg>


                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.41.12"><l><abbr>bha</abbr><ex>vati cātra</ex> ||
                            <caesura/>
                             guṇā ye uktā dravyeṣu śarīreṣv api tān viduḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             sthānavṛddhikṣayāt tasmād dehināṃ dravyahetukā iti || </l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.41.trailer"><l> || 41 ||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.1"><l> athāto rasaviśeṣavi<g ref="#newa-gap-filler"/><cb n="2"/>jñānīyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ
                            || </l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.3"><l> ākāśapavanadahanatoyabhūmiṣu yathāsaṅkhyam
                            ekottaraparivṛddhāḥ || śa <lb n="4"/> bdasparśarūparasagandhāḥ
                            || tasmād āpyo rasaḥ parasparānupraveśā<cb n="2"/>c ca
                            sarvveṣāṃ sānnidhyam asti utkarṣāt tu grahaṇaṃ | sa khalvāpyo
                            rasaḥ śeṣabhūtasaṃsarggādvidagdhaḥ ṣaḍvidho <lb n="5"/>bhavati
                            | tad yathā | madhuro 'mlo lavaṇaḥ kaṭukastiktaḥ kaṣāya iti |
                            tatrodakavāhulyātmadhuraḥ | toyāgnivāhulyādamlaḥ
                            bhūmyagnivāhulyāl lavaṇaḥ vāyvākāśa<lb n="6"/>vāhulyāttiktaḥ |
                            vāyvagnivāhulyātkaṭukaḥ pṛthivyanilavāhulyātkaṣāya i<cb n="2"/>ti || </l></lg>
                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.4"><l> tatra madhurāmlalavaṇā vātaghnāḥ |
                            madhuratiktakaṣāyāḥ pittaghnāḥ kaṭutiktakaṣāyāḥ śle<milestone unit="jpeg" facs="dscn3061 fol 085.jpg upper"/><pb facs="87" n="85v"/><lb n="1"/>ṣmaghnāḥ || </l></lg>
                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.5"><l> tatra vāyurātmaivātmā | pittamāgneyaṃ |
                            śleṣma saumya iti || </l></lg>
                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.6"><l> ta ete rasāḥ svayonivardhanā
                            anyayonipraśamanāś ca | </l></lg>
                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.7"><l> kecid āhur agnīṣomīyatvājjagataḥ | dvividhā
                            rasāḥ <lb n="2"/> saumyā āgneyāś ca | tatra
                            madhuratiktakaṣāyāḥ saumyāḥ dvividhā kaṭvamla<cb n="2"/>lavaṇā
                            āgneyāḥ | madhurāmlalavaṇāḥ snigdhā guravaś ca dvividhā
                            kaṭutiktakaṣāyā rūkṣā laghavaś ca | saumyāḥ <lb n="3"/> śītāḥ
                            | āgneyās tūṣṇāḥ | </l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.8.1"><l> tatra śaityarūkṣalāghavavaiṣadyaguṇala<cb n="2"/>kṣaṇo vāyuḥ | tasya samānayoniḥ kaṣāyo rasaḥ |
                            saumyaḥ śaityāc chaity am abhivardhayati | raukṣyād raukṣyaṃ
                                <lb n="4"/>lāghavāl lāghavaṃ vaiṣadyād vaiṣadyam iti || </l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.8.2"><l> auṣṇyataikṣṇyaraukṣyalāghavavaiṣa<cb n="2"/>dyaguṇalakṣaṇampittantasya samānayoniḥ kaṭuko rasaḥ
                            | saumya auṣṇyād auṣṇyam abhivardhayati | taikṣṇyā<lb n="5"/>t
                            taikṣṇyaṃ raukṣyād raukṣyaṃ lāghavāl lāghavaṃ vaiśadyād
                            vaiśadyam iti || </l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.8.3"><l>
                            <cb n="2"/>mādhuryasnehagauravaśaityapaicchilyaguṇalakṣaṇaḥ
                            śleṣmā tasya samānayo nirmadhuro rasaḥ | <lb n="6"/>saumya
                            mādhuryāt mādhuryam abhivardhayati | snehāt snehaṃ gauravād
                            gauravaṃ śaityāc chaityaṃ paicchilyāt paicchilyam iti ||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.8.4"><l>tasya punar vviparītaḥ kaṭuko rasaḥ |
                                <milestone unit="jpeg" facs="dscn3061                             fol 085.jpg lower"/><pb facs="87" n="86v"/><lb n="1"/>śleṣmaṇaḥ
                            pratyanīkatvāt | kaṭukyātmādhuryam abhibhavati | raukṣyāt
                            snehaṃ lāghavād gauravaṃ auṣṇyāt paicchity an tad etan
                            nidarśanamātram uktaṃ | </l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.9"><l> rasalakṣaṇam ata ūrdhvaṃ vakṣyāmaḥ || <lb n="2"/> tatra yaḥ paritoṣam utpādayati tarppayati
                                mukhopalepa<unclear reason="smudged">ñ</unclear> janaya<cb n="2"/>ti prahlādayati śleṣmāṇañ cābhivardhayati sa
                            madhuraḥ || yo dantaharṣam utpādayati | mukhāśrāvañ jana-<lb n="3"/>yati śraddhāñ cotpādayati so 'mlaḥ || yo
                            bhaktarucim utpādayati <cb n="2"/>| kaphaprasekañ janayati
                            mārddavañ copādayati sa lavaṇaḥ || yo jihvām udvejayati śiro
                            gṛhṇāti <lb n="4"/>nāsikāñ ca śrāvayati sa kaṭukaḥ || yo gale
                            śoṣam utpāda-<cb n="2"/>yati mukhavaiṣadyam utpādayati
                            bhaktaruciñ copādyati sa tiktaḥ || ya āsyam pariśoṣayati- <lb n="5"/> jihvāṃ stambhayati kaṇṭham badhnāti hṛdayam
                            pīḍayati sa kaṣāyaḥ || </l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.10.1"><l>
                            <cb n="2"/>rasaguṇān ata ūrdhvam vakṣyāmaḥ || tatra madhuro
                            raso raktamāṃsado 'sthimajjojaḥ śukravardhanaś cākṣuṣyaḥ <lb n="6"/>keśyauvalakṛtsandhānaḥ śoṇitaḥ śoṇitaprasādo
                            vālavṛddhakṣatakṣīṇahitaḥ ṣaṭpadapipīlikānāmiṣṭatamaḥ ||
                            tṛṣṇāmūrcchādāhaḥ praśamanaḥ | ṣaḍindriyaprasādanaś ceti |
                                <milestone unit="jpeg" facs="dscn3062 fol 086.jpg upper"/><pb facs="88" n="86v"/><lb n="1"/>sa evaṃguṇo 'py eka
                            evātyartham upayujyamānaḥ kāsaḥ śvāso
                            lasakavamathurvvadanamādhuryasvaropaghātakṛmigalagaṇḍān
                            āpādayati | tathārvvudaślīpadavastigudopalepābhiṣyandapra<lb n="2"/>bhṛtīn nayanavikārānn upajayati || </l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.10.2"><l> amlas tu jaraṇaḥ pācanapavana<cb n="2"/>nigrahaṇo nulomano vidāhī vahiḥśītaḥ kledanaḥ prāyaśo hṛdyaś
                            ceti | sa khalv evaṃgu<lb n="3"/>ṇo 'py eka evātyartham
                            upayujyamāno dantaharṣanayanasammīlanaro<cb n="2"/>masamvejanakaphavilāyanaśarīrapraśithilatām āpādayati |

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.10.3"><l> lavaṇas tu saṃśodhanaḥ pācano viśleṣaṇas
                            tarppaṇaḥ kledanaḥ śaithilyakṛt<lb n="5"/>

                                sarvvaśarīrāvayavamā<cb n="2"/>rddavakaraś ceti | sa khalu
                            evaṃguṇo 'py eka evātyartham upasevyamāno gātrakaṇḍūko tha
                            śophavaivarṇṇakaraḥ <lb n="6"/>svaropaghātendriyopatāpān
                            upajanayati |
                            tathākṣimukhapākaraktapittavātaśoṇitāmlīkāprabhṛtīnvikārān
                            upajanayati || </l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.10.4"><l> kaṭukas tu dīpanaḥ pācano rocanaḥ
                            śodhanaḥ sthaulyā<milestone unit="jpeg" facs="dscn3062 fol 086.jpg lower"/><pb facs="88" n="87r"/><lb n="1"/>lyālasakaviṣakuṣṭhakaṇḍūpraśamanaḥ
                            sandhibandhacchedano vasādanas stanyaśukrakaphamedasām
                            upahantā ceti | sa khalv evaṃguṇo 'py eka evātyartham
                                upayujyamā<lb n="2"/>no
                            bhramamadagalatālvoṣṭhaśoṣadāhasantāpān āpādayati | <cb n="2"/>tathā valavighātakampatodabhedakṛt
                            karacaraṇapārśvapṛṣṭhaprabhṛtiṣu ca vātaśūlān upajanayati || </l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.10.5"><l> ti<lb n="3"/>ktas tu rocano dīpanaḥ
                            śodhanaḥ kaṇḍūko<unclear reason="faded">ṣṭha</unclear>tṛṣṇāmūrcchāpraśanaḥ <cb n="2"/>stanyaśodhano

                            'py eka evātyartham u<lb n="4"/>payujyamāno
                            gātramanyāstambhākṣepakordditaśiraḥśūlān upa-<cb n="2"/>janayati | tathā bhramatodabhedacchedāsyavairasyāny
                            āpādayati || </l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.10.6"><l>kaṣāyas tu saṅgrāhiko ropaṇaḥ stambha<lb n="5"/>naḥ śodhanollekhanaḥ śoṣaṇaḥ pīḍanaḥ kledopaśoṣaṇaś
                            ceti | sa <cb n="2"/>khalv evaṅguṇo 'py eka evātyartham
                            upayujyamāno hṛdayapīḍāsyaśoṣodarādhmānavākyagraha<lb n="6"/>manyāstambhaprabhṛtīn vikārān upajanayati | tathā
                            gātrasphuraṇacimicimāyanākuñcanākṣepaṇaprabhṛtīn
                            vikārānupajanayati || </l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.11"><l> sarvveṣām eva dravyāṇy upadekṣyā<milestone unit="jpeg" facs="dscn3063 fol .jpg upper"/><pb facs="89" n="87v"/><lb n="1"/>maḥ | tad yathā kākolyādi
                            kṣīraghṛtavasāmajjaśāliyavagodhūmamāṣaśṛṅgāṭakakaśeruka
                                kāl<unclear>āṅkā</unclear>lukapiyālapuṣkaravījakāśmaryamadhūkadrākṣākharjūrarājādananā<lb n="2"/>likerekṣuvikārāḥ |
                                balātibalātmaguptāvidārīpayasyā<cb n="2"/>gokṣurakakṣīramoraṭamadhūlikākūṣmāṇḍādiḥ samāsena madhuro
                            varggaḥ || dāḍimāmalakamātu<lb n="3"/>luṅgāmrāmrātakakapitthakaramarddavadaraprācīnāmalakakośā<cb n="2"/>mrabhavyapārāvatavetraphalatintiḍīkalajacāmlavetasadantaśaṭhadadhitakrasurāsauvīratuṣodaka<lb n="4"/>dhānyāmlaprabhṛtīni samāsenāmlo varggaḥ ||
                                saindhavasauvarccalavi-<cb n="2"/>ḍapākyaromakasāmudrapācimakṣāroṣarasuvarccilaprabhṛtīni
                            samāsena lavaṇo varggaḥ || pippa-<lb n="4"/>lyādiḥ surasādir
                                mmadhuśigrumūlakalaśunasumukhaśītaśi<cb n="2"/>vakuṣṭhadevadāruhareṇukā
                                'valgujaphalacaṇḍāguggulumustalāṅgalakīśukanāsīpīluprabhṛtī-<lb n="5"/>ni śālasārādiś ca prāyaśaḥ kaṭuko varggaḥ ||
                                āragvadhādirgguḍūcyādirmmaṇḍū<add place="lower margin"/>ka<add/>parṇṇīvetrakarīraharidrādvayendrayavavaruṇasvādukaṇṭakasaptaparṇṇavṛhatīdvaya<milestone unit="jpeg" facs="dscn3063 fol 087.jpg lower"/><pb facs="89" n="88r" ana="facs 90 duplicates facs 89"/>śaṃkhinītṛvṛtkṛtavedhanakarkkoṭakakāravellavārttākukaravīrasumanāḥśaṃkhapuṣpyapāmārggatrāyamāṇāśokarohivaijayantīsuvarccalāvṛścikālījyotiṣmatīprabhṛtīni
                            tikto va<lb n="2"/>rggaḥ || nyagrodhādirambaṣṭhādir lodhrādiḥ
                            priyaṅvādistriphalā śalla<cb/>kījamvvasthitindukādīni
                            katakaśākaphalāni sālasārādiś ca prāyasaḥ
                                kuravakakovidārajīvantī<lb n="3"/>suniṣaṇṇakaprabhṛtīni
                            varakādayo mudgādayaś ca samāsena kaṣā-<cb/>yo varggaḥ || </l></lg>
                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.12"><l> tatraiteṣāṃ rasānāṃ saṃyogās triṣaṣṭirv
                            bhavanti || tad yathā pañcadaśa dvikāḥ | viṃśatis trikāḥ | <lb n="4"/>pañcadaśa catuṣkāḥ | ṣaṭ pañcakāḥ | ekaṣaṭkāḥ |
                            ekaikaśaś ca śaḍrasā <cb/>iti | teṣām anyatra vakṣyāmaḥ || </l></lg>
                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.42.13"><l> bhavati cātra || yuktāḥ ṣaḍadhigacchanti
                            valino vaśyatāṃ rasāḥ | yathā doṣā<lb n="5"/>ḥ prakupitā vaśaṃ

                    <div n="43" type="adhyāya">

                        <milestone facs="dscn3063 fol 087.jpg" n="88r5" unit="folio"/>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.43.1"><l> athāto vamanadravyavikalpavijñānīyam
                            vyākhyāsyāmaḥ ||</l></lg>



                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.43.3.part1"><l> vamanadravyānām phalākhyānām madhye
                                madanapha<g ref="#newa-gap-filler"/><lb n="6"/>lāni
                            śreṣṭhatamāni bhavanti || tatra madanapuṣpāṇām ātapaśuṣkāṇā
                            cūrṇaprakuñcaṃ pratyak puṣpīnimvakaṣāyāṇām
                            anyatamenālo<unclear/> pāyayitvā vāmayet |
                                madanaśatvāṭūcūrṇṇā<milestone unit="jpeg" facs="dscn3065 fol 088.jpg upper"/><pb facs="91" n="88v"/><lb n="1"/>ny evam madhulavaṇayuktāny abhiprataptāni |
                            madanaśalāṭucūrṇṇasiddhām vā tilayavāgūn | nivṛttām vā
                            nātihariti pāṇḍūnāṃ kuśamūḍhāvavaddhamṛdgomayapraliptānāṃ
                                yavavūsamāṣa<g ref="#newa-gap-filler"/>śālyādidhānyarāśāvaṣṭharātroṣitaklinnabhinnānām
                            phalapi<cb/>ppalīrāhṛtyātape pariśoṣayet tāsāṃ
                            dadhipalavimṛditapariśuṣkāṇāṃ subhājanasthānām antarnnakha<lb n="3"/>muṣṭhim uṣṇena yaṣṭhīmadhukaṣāyeṇa
                                kovidārādhyanyatama<unclear>kā</unclear>ṣā<cb/>yeṇa vā
                            vimṛdya trirātraparyuṣitam madhusaindhavayuktam āśīrbhir
                            abhimantritam udaṅmukhaḥ prāṅmukham ā<g ref="#newa-gap-filler"/><lb n="4"/>turam pāyayet anena mantreṇa abhimantrya

                             peyalehyādyabhakṣyāṇāṃ gurūvidyād yathotta<lb n="3"/>raṃ || </l></lg>


                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.495"><l> gurūṇām adhasauhity aṃ lafhūnāṃ vṛttir
                            iṣyate |
                            <caesura/>
                             dravottaro dra<cb/>vaś cāpi na mātrā gurur iṣyate || </l></lg>


                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.496"><l> dravādyam aviśuṣkan tu samyakkāny
                            upapadyate |
                            <caesura/>
                             viśuṣkam annam abhyastan na pā<lb n="4"/>kaṃ sādhu gacchati
                            || </l></lg>


                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.497"><l> piṇḍīkṛtasamaṃ klinnam vidāham
                            upagacchati <cb/> |
                            <caesura/>
                             śrotasy annavahe pittam paktau vā yasya tiṣṭhati || </l></lg>


                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.498"><l> vidāhi bhuktam anyad vā tasyāpy annam
                            vidahyate |
                            <caesura/>
                             śuṣkam bhuktam vi<lb n="5"/>dagdhaṃ <unclear>ḥ</unclear>
                            syādagdher vyāpādakārakaḥ || </l></lg>


                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.499"><l> āmam vidadham viṣṭac ca
                            kapha<cb/>pittānilais tribhiḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             ajīrṇṇe kecid icchanti caturthaṃ rasaśeṣataḥ || </l></lg>


                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.500"><l> atyamvupānād viṣam āśanāc ca sandhā<lb n="6"/>raṇāt svapnaviparyayād vā |
                            <caesura/>
                             kāle 'pi sātmyaṃ laghu cāpi bhuktam annan na pākaṃ bhajate
                            narasya || </l></lg>

                        <!--  
                    Harshal Bhatt
                        Missing verses : 522, 523ab,
                        Addition : 503cd, 531, 532
                        Notes : 514 - 519 are given after 519 
                    
 -->

                        <!--  
  500 not here 
 -->

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.502"><l>mādhuryam annaṅgatamāṃsasaṃjñām
                            vidagdhasaṃjñāṃ gatam amlabhāvaṃ |
                            <caesura/>
                             kiñcid vidagdhaṃ bhṛśatodaśūlam vi<milestone unit="jpeg" facs="dscn3100 fol 122.jpg upper"/><pb facs="126" n="122v"/><lb n="1"/>ṣṭabdham ānaddhaniruddhavātaṃ ||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.503"><l>udgāraśuddhāvapi bhakṣakāṃkṣā na jāyate
                            hṛdgurutā ca yasya |
                            <caesura/>
                             rasāvaśeṣeṇa tu saprasekaṃ caturtham etan pravadanty ajīrṇṇaṃ
                            ||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.504"><l>mūrcchā pralāpo vamathuḥ<lb n="2"/>praseko
                            jvaro 'tisāraḥ sadanam bhramaś ca || śirorujā
                            pṛṣṭhaka<cb/>ṭigrahaś ca
                            <caesura/>
                             tṛṣṇāvipāko 'tha vijṛmbhikā ca || upadravā bhavanty ete
                            maraṇam vāpy ajīrṇṇataḥ |</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.505"><l>tatrāme <lb n="3"/>laṃghanaṅ kāryam
                            vidagdhe vamanaṃ hitaṃ ||
                            <caesura/>
                             viṣṭabdhe svedanaṃ śastaṃ<cb/> rasaśeṣe śayīta ca |</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.506"><l>vāmayed āśu tan tasmād uṣṇena lavaṇāmvunā
                            ||
                            <caesura/>
                             kāryan cānaśanan tāvad yāvan na pra<lb n="4"/>kṛtim vrajet
                            |</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.507"><l>laghukāyamanaś cainaṃ laghvannaiḥ
                            samupācaret ||<cb/>
                            <caesura/>
                             yāvan na prakṛtisthaḥ syād doṣataḥ ghrāṇatas tathā |</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.508"><l>hitāhitopasaṃyuktam anna<pc> </pc><unclear reason="corrections to anusvaras">saṃśamanaṃ</unclear>
                            smṛtaṃ ||
                            <caesura/>
                             vahu<lb n="5"/> stokam akāle vā taṃ jñeyam viṣamāśanaṃ |</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.509"><l>sājīrṇṇe bhujyate ya<cb/>s tu tad
                            adhyaśanam ucyate ||
                            <caesura/>
                             tadvad enan nihanty āśu vahūn vyādhīn karoti ca ||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.510"><l>annam vidagdhaṃ hi narasya<lb n="6"/>śīghraṃ śītāmvunā vai paripākam eti |
                            <caesura/>
                             taddhyasya śaity ena nihanti pittam ākledibhāvāc ca na yānty
                            adhastāt ||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.511"><l>vidahyate yasya tu bhuktamātraṃ <milestone unit="jpeg" facs="dscn3100 fol 122.jpg lower"/><pb facs="126" n="123r"/><lb n="1"/>dahyanti hṛtkoṣṭhagalām̐ś
                            ca yasya |
                            <caesura/>
                             drākṣāsitāṃ mākṣikasaṃprayuktā līḍhvābhayām vai sasukhaṃ
                            labheta ||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.512"><l>bhaved ajīrṇṇaṃ prati yasya śaṅkā
                            snigdhasya jantor vvalino 'nnakāle |
                            <caesura/>
                             prātaḥ saśu<lb n="2"/>ṇṭhīm abhayām aśaṅko bhuñjīta samprāśya
                            hitaṃ hitārthī ||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.513"><l>svalpaṃ<cb/> yadā doṣavivandhamāmaṃ līnan
                            na tejaḥ patham āvṛṇoti |
                            <caesura/>
                             bhavanty ajīrṇṇe 'pi tadā vubhukṣā sā ma<lb n="3"/>ndavṛddhim
                            viṣavan nihanti ||<unclear extent="1" unit="syllable"/></l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.514"><l>ata ūrddhvam pravakṣyāmi<cb/> guṇānāṅ
                            karmmavistaraṃ |
                            <caesura/>
                             karmmabhis tv anumīyante nānā dravyāśrayā guṇāḥ ||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.519cd"><l>daśādyāḥ karmmataḥ proktā<lb n="4"/>s
                            teṣāṅ karmmaviśeṣaṇaiḥ |</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.520ab"><l>daśaivādyām pravakṣyāmi dravādīs
                            tām̐<cb/>ś chṛṇuśva me ||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.515"><l>śītaḥ prahlādanastambhī
                            mūrcchātṛṭsvedadāhajit ||1||
                            <caesura/>
                             uṣṇastad viparītaḥ syāt pāca<lb n="5"/>naś ca viśeṣataḥ
                            ||2||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.516"><l>sneho mārddavakṛt snigdho
                            valavarṇṇa<cb/>karas tathā ||3||
                            <caesura/>
                             rūkṣas tad viparītaḥ syād viśeṣāt stambhanaḥ smṛtaḥ ||4||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.517"><l>picchilo jīvanaśle<lb n="6"/>ṣī sandhāno
                            vṛṃhaṇas tathā ||5||
                            <caesura/>
                             viṣado viparīto 'sya bhedī śodhanaropaṇaḥ ||6||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.518"><l>dāhapākakaras tīkṣṇaḥ śrāvaṇo mṛdur
                            anyathā ||8||
                            <caesura/>
                             sāndropalepaḥ kaphakṛ<milestone unit="jpeg" facs="dscn3101 fol 123.jpg upper"/><pb facs="127" n="123v"/><lb n="1"/>d guruḥ prīṇanavṛṃhaṇaḥ ||10||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.519ab"><l>laghus tad viparītaḥ syāl lekhano
                            ropaṇas tathā ||11||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.520cd"><l>dravaḥ prakledanaḥ sāndraḥ śuṣkaḥ syād
                            dvandvakārakaḥ ||13||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.521"><l>ślakṣṇaḥ picchilavaj jñeyaḥ ka<lb n="2"/>rkkaśo viṣado yathā ||15||
                            <caesura/>
                             sukhānuvarttī sūkṣmañ ca sugandhī<cb/>rocano mataḥ ||16||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.522"><l>durggandho viparīto sya prakāśau cāpy
                            ubhāv api ||17||
                            <caesura/>
                             saro 'nulomanaḥ<lb n="3"/> prokto mando yātrākaraḥ smṛtaḥ
                            ||19||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.523"><l> vyavāyī deham akhilaṃ<cb/> vyāpya pākāya
                            kalpate ||20||</l></lg>


                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.525"><l>guṇā viṃśatir ity ete karmmataḥ
                            parikīrttitāḥ ||<unclear unit="syllable"/></l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.526"><l>ata ūrddham pravakṣyāmi āhāragatiniścayaṃ
                            |
                            <caesura/>
                             pañcabhūtātmake de<cb/>he āhāraḥ pākabhautikaḥ ||
                            <caesura/>
                             vipakvaḥ pañcadhā samyag guṇām̐s tān abhivarddhayet ||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.527"><l>avidagdhaṃ kaphaṃ pittam vidagdhaḥ pavanam
                            punaḥ ||
                            <caesura/>
                             samyag vipakṣo niḥsāra āhāraḥ<cb/> parivṛṃhayet |</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.528"><l>viṇmūtram āhāramalaḥ sāraḥ prāgīrito rasaḥ
                            ||
                            <caesura/>
                             sa tu vyānena vikṣiptaḥ sarvvān dhā<lb n="6"/>tūn visarppati
                            |</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.529"><l>kapha pitta malaḥ kheṣu svedaḥ syān
                            nakharoma ca ||
                            <caesura/>
                             netraviṭ tvakṣu ca sneho dhātūnāṃ kramaśo malāḥ |</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.530"><l>divā vivuddhahṛdaye jāgrataḥ puṇḍarīkavat
                            ||
                            <caesura/>
                             anupakli<milestone unit="jpeg" facs="dscn3101 fol 123.jpg lower"/><pb facs="127" n="124r"/><lb n="1"/>nna dhātvannam ajīrṇṇepi hitaṃ niśi |</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.531"><l>hṛdayaṃ līyate rātrau prasuptasya
                            viśeṣataḥ ||
                            <caesura/>
                             samupaklinnadhātvannam ajīrṇṇe tvahitaṃ divā ||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.532"><l>imam vidhiṃ yo 'numatam mahāmuner
                                mmaharṣimu<lb n="2"/>khyasya paṭhet tu yatnataḥ |
                            <caesura/>
                             sa bhūmipāloya vidhātum auṣadham mahā<cb/>tmanāñ cārhati
                            vaidyasattama iti || o ||</l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.532.add1"><l><add place="inline">dravyajñānaṃ
                                rasajñānam vasanañ ca virecanaṃ |
                                <caesura/>
                                 dravadravya<lb n="3"/>parijñānam annapānena ṣaṭ smṛtaḥ ||
                                o ||</add></l></lg>

                        <lg xml:id="SS.1.46.532.add2"><l><add place="inline">sūtrasthāne
                                purā<cb/> proktañ catvāriṃśat ṣaḍuttaraṃ |
                                <caesura/>
                                 adhyāyāḥ kāśirājena pūrṇṇaṃ sarvvaṃ savistaraṃ
                            ||</add></l></lg>
